#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

codex
The matrix has a fixed dense input vector and independent output rows, so I’m using a contiguous CSR row decomposition balanced by actual nonzeros. Each rank will retain only its local matrix rows, while the vector is broadcast once; the timed iteration loop then has no communication, and only rank 0 gathers output when validation or result printing requires it. This preserves each row’s original summation order and therefore its numerical semantics.

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
 }

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
 }

+// hot loop is identical to the original serial row kernel.
+void spmvCpu(const double* SPMV_RESTRICT val,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT cols,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT rowDelimiters,
+             const double* SPMV_RESTRICT vec, const index_t rows,

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
 }

+// hot loop is identical to the original serial row kernel.
+void spmvCpu(const double* SPMV_RESTRICT val,

+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT rowDelimiters,
+             const double* SPMV_RESTRICT vec, const index_t rows,

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
 }

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
 }

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
 }

+// hot loop is identical to the original serial row kernel.
+void spmvCpu(const double* SPMV_RESTRICT val,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT cols,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT rowDelimiters,

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
 }

+// Compute the rank-local rows.  The local row delimiters start at zero, so the
+// hot loop is identical to the original serial row kernel.
+void spmvCpu(const double* SPMV_RESTRICT val,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT cols,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT rowDelimiters,
+             const double* SPMV_RESTRICT vec, const index_t rows,
+             double* SPMV_RESTRICT out) noexcept {
+    for (index_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[i + 1];

+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
 }

+// Compute the rank-local rows.  The local row delimiters start at zero, so the
+// hot loop is identical to the original serial row kernel.
+void spmvCpu(const double* SPMV_RESTRICT val,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT cols,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT rowDelimiters,
+             const double* SPMV_RESTRICT vec, const index_t rows,
+             double* SPMV_RESTRICT out) noexcept {
+    for (index_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[i + 1];
+        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < end; ++j) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];

-        out[i] = t;
+        out[i] = sum;
     }

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
 }

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
+}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
+}
+

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
+}
+

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
+}

   353	    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? globalValues.data() : nullptr,
   354	                 nnzCounts.data(), nnzDisplacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
   355	                 localValues.data(), nnzCounts[rank], MPI_DOUBLE,
   356	                 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   357	
   358	    const index_t localBase = firstNnz[rank];
   359	    for (index_t& delimiter : localRowDelimiters) {
   360	        delimiter -= localBase;
   361	    }
   362	
   363	    // Release the global matrix before benchmarking; rank zero participates
   364	    // with the same local-only representation as every other process.
   365	    if (rank == 0) {
   366	        std::vector<double>().swap(globalValues);
   367	        std::vector<index_t>().swap(globalColumns);
   368	        std::vector<index_t>().swap(globalRowDelimiters);
   369	        std::printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
   370	    }
   371	
   372	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   373	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   374	    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
   375	        spmvCpu(localValues.data(), localColumns.data(), localRowDelimiters.data(),
   376	                vector.data(), localRows, localOutput.data());
   377	    }
   378	    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   379	
   380	    double elapsedSeconds = 0.0;
   381	    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &elapsedSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   382	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   383	
   384	    if (rank == 0) {
   385	        const double averageMs = iterations == 0
   386	            ? 0.0 : elapsedSeconds * 1000.0 / iterations;
   387	        const double gflops = elapsedSeconds == 0.0
   388	            ? 0.0
   389	            : (2.0 * static_cast<double>(nItems) * iterations) /
   390	                  elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9;
   391	        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
   392	        std::printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", averageMs);
   393	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   394	    }
   395	
   396	    std::vector<double> globalOutput;
   397	    if (validate || printResults) {
   398	        if (rank == 0) {
   399	            globalOutput.resize(numRows);
   400	        }
   401	        MPI_Gatherv(localOutput.data(), outputCounts[rank], MPI_DOUBLE,
   402	                    rank == 0 ? globalOutput.data() : nullptr,
   403	                    outputCounts.data(), outputDisplacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
   404	                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   405	    }
   406	
   407	    int exitStatus = 0;
   408	    if (rank == 0) {
   409	        if (printResults) {
   410	            print_results(globalOutput, "OutputVector");
   411	        }
   412	        if (validate) {
   413	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   414	            const bool valid = verifyResults(reference.data(), globalOutput.data(), numRows);
   415	            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   416	            exitStatus = valid ? 0 : 1;
   417	        }
   418	    }
   419	
   420	    MPI_Bcast(&exitStatus, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   421	    MPI_Finalize();
   422	    return exitStatus;
   423	}

    17	constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
    18	
    19	#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    20	#define SPMV_RESTRICT __restrict__
    21	#else
    22	#define SPMV_RESTRICT

    24	
    25	// Initialize an array with the same random-number sequence as the original
    26	// serial benchmark.
    27	void fill(double* array, const index_t size, const double maxVal) {
    28	    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
    29	        array[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    30	    }
    31	}
    32	
    33	// Build the original global CSR sparsity pattern on rank zero.  Initialization
    34	// is outside the timed region; the resulting rows are distributed afterwards.
    35	void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n,
    36	                      const index_t dim) {
    37	    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;
    38	    const double prob = static_cast<double>(n) /
    39	                        (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));
    40	
    41	    srand(8675309);
    42	    bool fillRemaining = false;
    43	    const std::uint64_t entries = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(dim) * dim;
    44	    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
    45	        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;
    46	        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {
    47	            const std::uint64_t position = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(i) * dim + j;
    48	            const std::uint64_t numEntriesLeft = entries - position;
    49	            const index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;
    50	            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
    51	                fillRemaining = true;
    52	            }
    53	            const double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
    54	            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {
    55	                cols[nnzAssigned++] = j;
    56	            }
    57	        }
    58	    }
    59	    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;
    60	}
    61	
    62	// Compute the rank-local rows.  The local row delimiters start at zero, so the
    63	// hot loop is identical to the original serial row kernel.
    64	void spmvCpu(const double* SPMV_RESTRICT val,
    65	             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT cols,
    66	             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT rowDelimiters,
    67	             const double* SPMV_RESTRICT vec, const index_t rows,
    68	             double* SPMV_RESTRICT out) noexcept {
    69	    for (index_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
    70	        double sum = 0.0;
    71	        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[i + 1];
    72	        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < end; ++j) {
    73	            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
    74	        }
    75	        out[i] = sum;
    76	    }
    77	}
    78	
    79	bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
    80	    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
    81	        const double ref = reference[i];
    82	        const double res = result[i];
    83	        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
    84	            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
    85	                std::printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n",
    86	                            i, ref, res);
    87	                return false;
    88	            }
    89	        } else {
    90	            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
    91	            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
    92	                std::printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e "
    93	                            "(rel error: %.10e)\n",
    94	                            i, ref, res, relError);
    95	                return false;
    96	            }
    97	        }
    98	    }
    99	    return true;
   100	}

   107	    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");

   109	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");

   111	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   112	}
   113	
   114	bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
   115	    if (text[0] == '-') {
   116	        return false;
   117	    }
   118	    errno = 0;
   119	    char* end = nullptr;
   120	    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   121	    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
   122	        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
   123	        return false;
   124	    }
   125	    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
   126	    return true;
   127	}
   128	
   129	bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
   130	    errno = 0;
   131	    char* end = nullptr;
   132	    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
   133	    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
   134	        return false;
   135	    }
   136	    value = parsed;
   137	    return true;
   138	}
   139	
   140	// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
   141	// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
   142	// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
   143	std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
   144	                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
   145	                                   const int processCount) {
   146	    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
   147	    firstRow.front() = 0;
   148	    firstRow.back() = rows;
   149	
   150	    if (nonzeros == 0) {
   151	        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
   152	            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
   153	                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
   154	        }
   155	        return firstRow;
   156	    }
   157	
   158	    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
   159	        const std::uint64_t target =
   160	            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
   161	        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
   162	        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
   163	        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
   164	        if (high > rows) {
   165	            high = rows;
   166	        }
   167	        index_t chosen = high;
   168	        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
   169	            const index_t low = high - 1;
   170	            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
   171	            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
   172	            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
   173	                chosen = low;
   174	            }
   175	        }
   176	        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
   177	    }
   178	    return firstRow;
   179	}

   181	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   182	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   183	
   184	    int rank = 0;
   185	    int processCount = 1;
   186	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   187	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processCount);
   188	
   189	    index_t numRows = 1024;
   190	    index_t sparsity = 10;
   191	    index_t iterations = 10;
   192	    double maxVal = 1.0;
   193	    int validate = 0;
   194	    int printResults = 0;
   195	    // -1 means continue, 0 means help/success, and 1 means an argument error.
   196	    int startupStatus = -1;
   197	
   198	    if (rank == 0) {
   199	        for (int i = 1; i < argc && startupStatus == -1; ++i) {
   200	            const char* option = argv[i];
   201	            if (std::strcmp(option, "-n") == 0 || std::strcmp(option, "-s") == 0 ||
   202	                std::strcmp(option, "-i") == 0) {
   203	                index_t* destination = std::strcmp(option, "-n") == 0 ? &numRows
   204	                    : (std::strcmp(option, "-s") == 0 ? &sparsity : &iterations);
   205	                if (i + 1 >= argc || !parseIndex(argv[++i], *destination)) {
   206	                    std::fprintf(stderr, "Invalid value for %s\n", option);
   207	                    startupStatus = 1;
   208	                }
   209	            } else if (std::strcmp(option, "-m") == 0) {
   210	                if (i + 1 >= argc || !parseDouble(argv[++i], maxVal)) {
   211	                    std::fprintf(stderr, "Invalid value for -m\n");
   212	                    startupStatus = 1;
   213	                }
   214	            } else if (std::strcmp(option, "-v") == 0) {
   215	                validate = 1;
   216	            } else if (std::strcmp(option, "-r") == 0) {
   217	                printResults = 1;
   218	            } else if (std::strcmp(option, "-h") == 0) {
   219	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   220	                startupStatus = 0;
   221	            } else {
   222	                std::fprintf(stderr, "Unknown option: %s\n", option);
   223	                startupStatus = 1;
   224	            }
   225	        }
   226	
   227	        if (startupStatus == -1 && sparsity == 0) {
   228	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Sparsity must be greater than zero\n");
   229	            startupStatus = 1;
   230	        }
   231	
   232	        if (startupStatus == 1) {
   233	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   234	        }
   235	    }
   236	
   237	    MPI_Bcast(&startupStatus, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   238	    if (startupStatus != -1) {
   239	        MPI_Finalize();
   240	        return startupStatus;
   241	    }
   242	
   243	    MPI_Bcast(&numRows, 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   244	    MPI_Bcast(&sparsity, 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   245	    MPI_Bcast(&iterations, 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   246	    MPI_Bcast(&maxVal, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   247	    MPI_Bcast(&validate, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   248	    MPI_Bcast(&printResults, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   249	
   250	    const std::uint64_t matrixEntries = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(numRows) * numRows;
   251	    const std::uint64_t nItemsWide = matrixEntries / sparsity;
   252	    // This implementation uses the benchmark's 32-bit CSR indices and the
   253	    // widely supported MPI-3 collective interfaces, whose counts/displacements
   254	    // are signed ints.
   255	    if (nItemsWide > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max() ||
   256	        nItemsWide > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
   257	        numRows > static_cast<index_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max() - 1)) {
   258	        if (rank == 0) {
   259	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Problem size exceeds the supported 32-bit CSR/MPI range\n");
   260	        }

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
+}

+// hot loop is identical to the original serial row kernel.
+void spmvCpu(const double* SPMV_RESTRICT val,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT cols,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT rowDelimiters,
+             const double* SPMV_RESTRICT vec, const index_t rows,

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
+}

+// Compute the rank-local rows.  The local row delimiters start at zero, so the
+// hot loop is identical to the original serial row kernel.
+void spmvCpu(const double* SPMV_RESTRICT val,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT cols,
+             const index_t* SPMV_RESTRICT rowDelimiters,
+             const double* SPMV_RESTRICT vec, const index_t rows,
+             double* SPMV_RESTRICT out) noexcept {
+    for (index_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[i + 1];
+        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < end; ++j) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
         }
-        out[i] = t;
+        out[i] = sum;
     }
 }

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
+}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
+}
+

codex
Implemented MPI-based distributed SpMV while preserving the `spmv` executable name.

Key changes:

- Nonzero-balanced contiguous CSR row partitioning across MPI ranks.
- One-time dense-vector broadcast and CSR distribution.
- Communication-free timed iteration loop.
- Optional output gather only for validation or result printing.
- Maximum-rank computation timing using `MPI_Wtime` and `MPI_MAX`.
- Rank-zero-only initialization, reporting, and validation.
- Required MPI integration in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/spmv/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- Implementation in [spmv.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/spmv/spmv.cpp:183).

Verification completed:

- Clean Release build.
- Validation passed at 1, 2, 4, and 8 ranks.
- Output hashes exactly match the original serial implementation.
- Address/undefined-behavior sanitizer run passed.
- Measured near-linear scaling: 40.2 ms, 20.2 ms, and 10.4 ms at 1, 2, and 4 ranks.

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}
+
+bool parseDouble(const char* text, double& value) {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const double parsed = std::strtod(text, &end);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || !std::isfinite(parsed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = parsed;
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries whose cumulative nonzero counts are as
+// close as possible to equal shares.  Contiguity permits a zero-copy gather of
+// the final output while balancing the actual work rather than just row count.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t rows, const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> firstRow(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    firstRow.front() = 0;
+    firstRow.back() = rows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            firstRow[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rows) * rank) / processCount);
+        }
+        return firstRow;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank) / processCount;
+        const auto searchBegin = rowDelimiters.begin() + firstRow[rank - 1];
+        const auto highIt = std::lower_bound(searchBegin, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t high = static_cast<index_t>(highIt - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (high > rows) {
+            high = rows;
+        }
+        index_t chosen = high;
+        if (high > firstRow[rank - 1]) {
+            const index_t low = high - 1;
+            const std::uint64_t lowDistance = target - rowDelimiters[low];
+            const std::uint64_t highDistance = rowDelimiters[high] - target;
+            if (lowDistance <= highDistance) {
+                chosen = low;
+            }
+        }
+        firstRow[rank] = chosen;
+    }
+    return firstRow;
+}
+

Implemented MPI-based distributed SpMV while preserving the `spmv` executable name.

Key changes:

- Nonzero-balanced contiguous CSR row partitioning across MPI ranks.
- One-time dense-vector broadcast and CSR distribution.
- Communication-free timed iteration loop.
- Optional output gather only for validation or result printing.
- Maximum-rank computation timing using `MPI_Wtime` and `MPI_MAX`.
- Rank-zero-only initialization, reporting, and validation.
- Required MPI integration in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/spmv/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- Implementation in [spmv.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/spmv/spmv.cpp:183).

Verification completed:

- Clean Release build.
- Validation passed at 1, 2, 4, and 8 ranks.
- Output hashes exactly match the original serial implementation.
- Address/undefined-behavior sanitizer run passed.
- Measured near-linear scaling: 40.2 ms, 20.2 ms, and 10.4 ms at 1, 2, and 4 ranks.
